Covers: tune-once/cache/launch pattern, per-architecture configs (sm80–sm120), parameter space design (tile sizes, occupancy, num_ctas), and 7 common pitfalls with solutions. ([MiseOsBrigade/MiseOS](https://github.com/MiseOsBrigade/MiseOS/tree/HEAD/docs/source-library/icloud/mise-enhanced-gpu-pack-a-punch-v0.2.0-repo-upgrade.zip/mise-enhanced-gpu-pack-a-punch-workflow-machine/vendor/nv-skills/skills/TileGym/cutile-autotuning) · 2 copies)
- **cutile-dsl-ref**: &gt; ([PeppeRu96/hyper-mla](https://github.com/PeppeRu96/hyper-mla/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/cutile-dsl-ref))
- **cutile-fmha-attention-gb10**: cuTile can express the full FlashAttention/FMHA kernel in Python: load Q/K/V tiles, compute `QK^T`, apply online softmax with running `m` and `l` statistics, and accumulate the weighted sum. ([Ryukijano/agent-skills](https://github.com/Ryukijano/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/.cursor/skills/cutile-fmha-attention-gb10) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **cutile-guide**: Comprehensive guide for writing cuda.tile (cuTile) GPU kernels. Use when implementing, debugging, or reviewing impl_cutile.py files or any code using cuda.tile / ct_experimental. ([Deep-Learning-Profiling-Tools/Tilebench](https://github.com/Deep-Learning-Profiling-Tools/Tilebench/tree/HEAD/skills/cutile-guide) · ★14)
- **cutile-persistent-matmul-gb10**: Persistent kernels keep a tile block alive and process multiple output tiles in a strided loop. This is the dominant pattern for peak GEMM throughput in cuTile. ([Ryukijano/agent-skills](https://github.com/Ryukijano/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/.cursor/skills/cutile-persistent-matmul-gb10) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **cutile-python**: Expert cuTile programming assistant. Write high-performance GPU kernels using cuTile's tile-based programming model with proper validation and optimization. Supports deep agent orchestration for complex multi-kernel tasks. ([MiseOsBrigade/MiseOS](https://github.com/MiseOsBrigade/MiseOS/tree/HEAD/docs/source-library/icloud/mise-enhanced-gpu-pack-a-punch-v0.2.0-repo-upgrade.zip/mise-enhanced-gpu-pack-a-punch-workflow-machine/vendor/nv-skills/skills/TileGym/cutile-python) · 2 copies)
- **cutile-python-gb10**: cuTile is the new tile-based programming model introduced in CUDA 13. It lets you write GPU kernels in Python by operating on tiles (small arrays) instead of individual threads, and it can target Blackwell Tensor Cores and TMA without explicit PTX. ([Ryukijano/agent-skills](https://github.com/Ryukijano/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/.cursor/skills/cutile-python-gb10) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **cutile-reference**: Scope of this document: ([Deep-Learning-Profiling-Tools/Tilebench](https://github.com/Deep-Learning-Profiling-Tools/Tilebench/tree/HEAD/skills/reference/cutile/1.5.0) · ★14)
- **cutit**: Apply a token-efficiency protocol to agentic coding work — narrow before reading, delegate open-ended search, cap tool output, edit instead of rewrite — so the same task finishes on fewer tokens with no drop in correctness. Invoke at the start of any non-trivial coding task, or explicitly via /cutit. Load references/checklist.md before a large read/search/write when unsure if it's warranted; load references/techniques.md for the rationale behind a rule. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-core/skills/cutit) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-agent-reuse**: Catches spawning a brand-new sub-agent for follow-up work when an earlier one you already spawned is still idle and holds the exact context needed — a fresh spawn re-derives everything the idle one already knows. Invoke when a follow-up task relates to a sub-agent you spawned earlier in the same session. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-agents/skills/cutit-agent-reuse) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-artifact-reuse**: Catches regenerating an entire document, artifact, or file from scratch when the user asked for a small, localized tweak to one already produced. Invoke whenever a follow-up request modifies something already generated rather than asking for something new. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-runtime/skills/cutit-artifact-reuse) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-assertion-first**: Catches the pattern of generating a full solution before establishing what "correct" looks like, so a wrong turn only surfaces after a long elaboration has already been built on it. Invoke before writing a nontrivial solution, function, or multi-step answer whose correctness can be stated as a check up front. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-quality/skills/cutit-assertion-first) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-backend**: Catches backend-specific token waste — unbounded query results, full schema/log dumps, verbose stack traces past the first project frame, whole request/response bodies pulled in to check one field. Invoke when working on an API, database, server logs, or a migration. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-domains/skills/cutit-backend) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-background-task-batching**: Catches polling a long-running background task every few seconds "to stay on top of it" — invoke whenever you've just started a background process or agent and are deciding when to check it next. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-runtime/skills/cutit-background-task-batching) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-backoff-without-context-bloat**: Catches re-appending the same failed request/output/error block into context on every retry attempt during a backoff loop, letting an N-attempt retry cost N times the tokens of one. Invoke when writing or driving any retry-with-backoff loop. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-runtime/skills/cutit-backoff-without-context-bloat) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-batch-vs-realtime**: Catches bulk, non-urgent work (large-scale classification, log summarization, dataset transforms) being run through a real-time/interactive call path when a batch or async path would do the same job for less, and without occupying the interactive budget. Invoke when a task item-count is large and no immediate response is required. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-runtime/skills/cutit-batch-vs-realtime) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-breadth-vs-depth**: Catches running a broad multi-file exploration when the target is already well specified — a named file, function, or error message that points at one place. Invoke when the request names a specific symbol/file/path instead of describing an area to explore. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-runtime/skills/cutit-breadth-vs-depth) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-cache-aware-prompting**: Catches prompt structures that invalidate the provider's prefix cache on every call — invoke when assembling a prompt template that mixes stable instructions with per-call variables, especially in a loop that calls the same model repeatedly. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-tooling/skills/cutit-cache-aware-prompting) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-cheap-guardrail-first**: Catches sending every output through an expensive model-based safety/quality check when a cheap rule-based check (regex, schema validation, keyword list, length bound) would already catch most violations. Invoke when designing or running a guardrail pipeline that has both rule-based and model-based checks available. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-quality/skills/cutit-cheap-guardrail-first) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-cheap-precheck**: Catches the habit of launching a full test suite or manual end-to-end verification before running the lint/type-check/smoke-test that would have caught the same bug in seconds. Invoke before any expensive verification pass on a change that hasn't been syntax- or type-checked yet. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-quality/skills/cutit-cheap-precheck) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-checkpoint-planning**: Catches restarting a long multi-step task from step one after a mid-task failure, re-doing and re-verifying already-completed work instead of resuming from where it broke. Invoke when planning a long-running or many-step task that could fail partway through. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-agents/skills/cutit-checkpoint-planning) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-chunk-sizing**: Catches the reflex of retrieving maximal chunk sizes "to be safe" — invoke when configuring or calling a retrieval/chunking step and picking how much surrounding text to pull per hit. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-tooling/skills/cutit-chunk-sizing) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-citation-scoping**: Catches returning an entire source document when only the cited passage was needed — invoke whenever a retrieval or citation step is about to hand a full document back to the model instead of the specific span that supports the answer. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-tooling/skills/cutit-citation-scoping) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-clarify-before-commit**: Catches the pattern of guessing at an underspecified request and executing the full task on that guess, only to redo it at full cost once the user corrects the assumption. Invoke when a request has a genuine fork in interpretation before any code is written or files are searched. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-quality/skills/cutit-clarify-before-commit) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-config-reuse**: Catches re-querying environment or configuration facts (package manager, framework version, working directory, env vars) at every step of a task when the session already established them earlier. Invoke before any step that would re-check a fact already confirmed this session. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-runtime/skills/cutit-config-reuse) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-confirmation-batching**: Catches the pattern of asking the user to confirm one small decision at a time across several round-trips, when the decisions are known upfront and could be resolved in a single combined question. Invoke when a plan surfaces more than one point that needs user sign-off before or during execution. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-quality/skills/cutit-confirmation-batching) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-consensus-minimalism**: Catches the pattern of having cross-checking agents each re-derive a full independent solution when comparing compressed verdicts would settle agreement just as reliably. Invoke when multiple agents verify or vote on the same output and full re-derivation isn't what the comparison actually needs. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-agents/skills/cutit-consensus-minimalism) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-context-budget-tracking**: Catches running a long task without any awareness of remaining context headroom, so a hard limit forces an abrupt mid-task truncation — invoke periodically during any long-running or many-turn task. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-runtime/skills/cutit-context-budget-tracking) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-context-handoff**: Catches pasting the full parent transcript (or an entire file/log) into a sub-agent's prompt "so it has everything," when the sub-task only needs a fraction of it — the rest is pure re-processing cost paid by a model that has to read it before doing anything. Invoke when writing the prompt or briefing for a spawned sub-agent. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-agents/skills/cutit-context-handoff) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-context-injection-scoping**: Catches serializing an entire object (a full API response, a whole database row, a complete config struct) into context when the agent only needs a few fields from it — invoke when injecting structured data from code into a prompt. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-tooling/skills/cutit-context-injection-scoping) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-cross-turn-dedup**: Catches redoing a search, computation, or file read from an earlier turn because a later user message asks for something that's actually already been produced or is derivable from it. Invoke when a new request sounds similar to something already done earlier in the conversation. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-runtime/skills/cutit-cross-turn-dedup) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-delegation-threshold**: Catches indecision about whether to spawn a sub-agent, resolved by gut feel instead of a concrete rule — leading to either delegating trivial work (paying cold-start cost for nothing) or doing sprawling exploration inline (flooding your own context). Invoke at the moment of deciding whether a sub-task should be delegated. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-agents/skills/cutit-delegation-threshold) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-dependency-ordering**: Catches plans that run an expensive step before a cheap check that could have invalidated the whole plan — wasting the expensive step's tokens on a premise that was never going to hold. Invoke when ordering independent steps in a plan, before executing any of them. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-agents/skills/cutit-dependency-ordering) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-design**: Catches design-work token waste — reading a whole design system, style guide, or architecture doc to find one token or decision, re-describing a full Figma file or spec instead of its diff. Invoke when doing UI/UX design work (design tokens, components, Figma handoff) or system/architecture design (ADRs, design docs, API/interface contracts). ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-domains/skills/cutit-design) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-diff-only-output**: Catches printing an entire file or object's before-and-after state to show an edit — invoke whenever you're about to report a change and could show just the delta instead. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-quality/skills/cutit-diff-only-output) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-diff-verification**: Catches the reflex of re-reading an entire file to confirm an edit landed correctly when the diff itself already shows everything that changed. Invoke right after an edit, before deciding whether to re-read the file to check it. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-quality/skills/cutit-diff-verification) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-difficulty-based-routing**: Catches the default of running every subtask on the same top-tier model, even the mechanical ones (formatting, extraction, boilerplate scaffolding) that a cheaper model handles just as well. Invoke when a multi-step task contains subtasks whose difficulty clearly varies. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-runtime/skills/cutit-difficulty-based-routing) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-dynamic-prompt-assembly**: Catches shipping one static maximal prompt template that includes every possible section on every call — invoke when designing a prompt template that serves more than one task variant or mode. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-tooling/skills/cutit-dynamic-prompt-assembly) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-early-termination**: Catches running the remainder of a fixed plan on autopilot after the actual goal has already been satisfied by an earlier step — the leftover steps produce no additional value but still cost tokens. Invoke after any step in a multi-step plan, to check whether the original goal is already met. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-agents/skills/cutit-early-termination) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-error-triage-budget**: Catches pasting an entire stack trace, build log, or test-runner dump into context when only the last few frames or the first failing assertion are relevant. Invoke whenever a failing command's raw output is longer than a screen and hasn't been triaged yet. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-runtime/skills/cutit-error-triage-budget) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-escalation-on-failure**: Catches the reflex of defaulting straight to the most expensive model or highest-effort attempt "to be safe," when a cheap attempt would have succeeded most of the time. Invoke when starting a task whose difficulty is unknown and a cheap first try is verifiable. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-runtime/skills/cutit-escalation-on-failure) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-explore-then-commit**: Catches open-ended exploratory search that keeps going round after round looking for a "better" candidate instead of committing once a good-enough one turns up. Invoke when a search for a target (file, config, existing helper) has run more than two rounds without a clear miss. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-runtime/skills/cutit-explore-then-commit) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-external-state-over-recap**: Catches re-typing a natural-language summary of the plan or progress-so-far into the conversation each turn instead of holding it in structured task tracking. Invoke on any multi-step task where the current status would otherwise need restating in prose. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-runtime/skills/cutit-external-state-over-recap) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-fail-fast-guardrails**: Catches running expensive downstream pipeline steps before the cheapest guardrail check has had a chance to reject bad input, wasting every step that ran before the eventual rejection. Invoke when ordering a multi-stage pipeline that includes both cheap checks and expensive processing steps. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-quality/skills/cutit-fail-fast-guardrails) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-failure-scoped-logging**: Catches capturing an entire test/CI run's logs when only the failing case's slice is needed to diagnose it. Invoke when pulling logs after a run that had both passing and failing parts. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-runtime/skills/cutit-failure-scoped-logging) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-fan-in-summarization**: Catches reading each returned sub-agent result in full one at a time and re-summarizing after every one, instead of collecting all results and synthesizing once — repeated partial summarization re-processes the same growing context on every pass. Invoke when multiple sub-agents spawned in parallel are returning results that need to be combined. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-agents/skills/cutit-fan-in-summarization) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-few-shot-trimming**: Catches padding a prompt with more or longer few-shot examples than the task needs to hit target accuracy — invoke when assembling or reviewing a few-shot prompt template. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-tooling/skills/cutit-few-shot-trimming) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-frontend**: Catches frontend-specific token waste — reading compiled/bundled output instead of source, un-filtered devtool/console logs, whole-stylesheet reads to find one rule, full-page DOM dumps to verify a small visual change. Invoke when working on UI components, styles, or a frontend build/dev-server issue. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-domains/skills/cutit-frontend) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-function-calling-minimalism**: Catches tool/function signatures carrying optional parameters, verbose descriptions, or redundant fields that get paid on every single call — invoke when designing or reviewing a tool schema before it ships. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-tooling/skills/cutit-function-calling-minimalism) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-goal-scoping**: Catches over-delivering on a vague request — reading, searching, or building far more than the ask requires because the boundary was never pinned down. Invoke when a request is ambiguous in scope, before starting any exploration or execution. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-agents/skills/cutit-goal-scoping) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-golden-path-testing**: Catches the pattern of exhaustively verifying edge cases before confirming the primary path even works, spending tokens on inputs nobody has hit yet while the common case sits unchecked. Invoke when deciding what to verify first after implementing a change. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-quality/skills/cutit-golden-path-testing) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-graceful-degradation**: Catches escalating spend (bigger model, more retries, deeper search) chasing an ideal approach that's failing, when a cheaper fallback would satisfy the actual requirement. Invoke when a preferred method has failed once or twice and a simpler alternative exists. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-runtime/skills/cutit-graceful-degradation) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-handoff-summarization**: Catches the pattern of forwarding a raw transcript between agents or pipeline stages instead of a compressed summary of what the receiving side actually needs. Invoke at every agent-to-agent or stage-to-stage handoff before deciding what to pass along. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-agents/skills/cutit-handoff-summarization) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-idle-agent-reuse**: Catches spawning a brand-new sub-agent for a follow-up task when a recently completed or paused agent already holds the relevant context — invoke before any Agent call, to check for a resumable prior agent first. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-runtime/skills/cutit-idle-agent-reuse) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-incremental-validation**: Catches the pattern of running every step of a multi-step task back to back and only checking output at the end, where an early failure has already compounded through later steps built on it. Invoke when executing a task with more than two sequential steps, each depending on the last. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-quality/skills/cutit-incremental-validation) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-instruction-dedup**: Catches the same instruction being stated in the system prompt, repeated in the user message, and repeated again in a tool description — invoke when assembling a multi-message prompt (system + user + tool schemas) for a task. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-tooling/skills/cutit-instruction-dedup) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-known-failure-shortcuts**: Catches re-diagnosing an error from scratch when its signature (message, error code, stack shape) matches one already solved earlier this session or documented in the project. Invoke as soon as an error message appears — check it against known signatures before starting fresh investigation. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-runtime/skills/cutit-known-failure-shortcuts) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-known-location-shortcuts**: Catches searching for something that has a well-known conventional location instead of going straight there — config files, migration folders, entry points, CI definitions. Invoke when the target is a project-convention artifact rather than arbitrary user code. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-runtime/skills/cutit-known-location-shortcuts) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-lazy-tool-loading**: Catches the habit of resolving a deferred tool's full schema the moment it's mentioned, instead of at call time — invoke when a tool listing shows names-only entries you don't need yet. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-tooling/skills/cutit-lazy-tool-loading) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-long-task-progress-compaction**: Catches keeping the full detail of a finished phase in context after it's no longer actionable — invoke right after any phase of a multi-step task completes, before moving to the next one. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-runtime/skills/cutit-long-task-progress-compaction) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-memory-scoping**: Catches pulling an entire memory store into context when a query only needs the entries relevant to this turn — invoke whenever a memory/notes/preferences system is queried. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-runtime/skills/cutit-memory-scoping) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-memory-write-discipline**: Catches writing everything learned during a task into persistent memory, including facts trivially re-derivable from the code or logs already in the repo. Invoke before any write to a persistent memory/notes file. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-runtime/skills/cutit-memory-write-discipline) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-metric-first-triage**: Catches opening individual failing cases one by one before checking what the aggregate metrics already say about where the problem concentrates. Invoke when a run produces both a metrics summary and per-case detail, before drilling into any single case. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-quality/skills/cutit-metric-first-triage) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-multi-hop-retrieval-budget**: Catches unbounded chaining in multi-hop retrieval loops — invoke when a retrieval step's output is about to trigger another retrieval step ("follow this reference," "look up what X depends on") without a hop cap. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-tooling/skills/cutit-multi-hop-retrieval-budget) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-multi-session-continuity**: Catches persisting a full transcript or exploration log for a future session to read back in, when only a handful of durable facts are actually needed to pick the work back up — invoke when ending a session on a task that will continue later. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-runtime/skills/cutit-multi-session-continuity) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-negative-result-shortcut**: Catches exhaustively checking every remaining location to "prove" a negative when the question was already answered by the first authoritative miss. Invoke when a search is being used to confirm something does NOT exist, isn't referenced, or isn't the cause. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-runtime/skills/cutit-negative-result-shortcut) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-no-restate**: Catches opening a response by paraphrasing the user's request or re-summarizing a plan that was just agreed on — invoke right before the first sentence of any answer, especially after a plan or clarifying exchange. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-quality/skills/cutit-no-restate) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-orchestrator-lean**: Catches an orchestrator that delegates a sub-task but then also independently reads the same files or re-derives the same findings itself "just to be sure" — the work ends up held in two contexts instead of one. Invoke whenever you delegate a task, immediately after the sub-agent is spawned or has returned. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-agents/skills/cutit-orchestrator-lean) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-parallel-delegation**: Catches spawning independent sub-agents one at a time, waiting for each to finish before starting the next, when they don't depend on each other — serial spawns cost the same total tokens but add wall-clock and re-orientation overhead each time you resume. Invoke when a plan has two or more sub-tasks that don't depend on each other's output. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-agents/skills/cutit-parallel-delegation) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-partial-retry**: Catches re-running an entire multi-step task from step one after only the last step failed, redoing work that already succeeded. Invoke when a pipeline, script, or plan with multiple steps fails partway through. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-runtime/skills/cutit-partial-retry) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-pipeline-caching**: Catches the pattern of recomputing a pipeline stage's output on every run even when none of that stage's inputs changed since the last run. Invoke when a pipeline re-executes on repeated or incremental invocations and some stage's inputs are frequently unchanged. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-agents/skills/cutit-pipeline-caching) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-pipeline-short-circuit**: Catches the pattern of running every downstream stage of a pipeline even when an early stage's own check already fully answers the request. Invoke when designing pipeline control flow, or when a stage's output would make the remaining stages' work moot. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-agents/skills/cutit-pipeline-short-circuit) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-pipeline-stage-isolation**: Catches the pattern of forwarding a pipeline's entire upstream history into each new stage when that stage only needs the immediately prior output. Invoke when designing or wiring a multi-stage agent pipeline where later stages receive more than the previous stage's result. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-agents/skills/cutit-pipeline-stage-isolation) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-plan-caching**: Catches re-deriving a plan from first principles for a task shape you've already solved this session (or that the project defines a known recipe for) — the plan structure is reusable even when the specifics differ. Invoke when starting a task that matches a pattern you (or the project) have already planned before. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-agents/skills/cutit-plan-caching) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-plan-revision**: Catches the reflex of throwing out an entire multi-step plan and regenerating it from zero the moment scope shifts slightly — most of the original plan is still valid and re-deriving it is wasted tokens. Invoke whenever new information changes a plan that's already in progress or already written down. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-agents/skills/cutit-plan-revision) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-precomputed-index-reuse**: Catches re-scanning or re-summarizing the same codebase/document set for every new query in a session instead of building one index or summary up front and querying it repeatedly. Invoke when a session is likely to run multiple searches or questions over the same material. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-runtime/skills/cutit-precomputed-index-reuse) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-preference-capture**: Catches re-asking or re-deriving a preference the user already stated once (formatting style, tone, preferred tool, naming convention), instead of writing it to memory/config and reusing it silently in future turns and sessions. Invoke the moment a user states a durable preference, not just when you need it again. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-quality/skills/cutit-preference-capture) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-proactive-compaction**: Catches leaving a fully resolved sub-thread (a debugging detour, a completed subtask) sitting in full detail in context instead of summarizing it once it's done — invoke right after any sub-thread reaches a conclusion. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-runtime/skills/cutit-proactive-compaction) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-progressive-widening**: Catches defaulting to a repo-wide search when a narrower scope (current directory, current module, file type matching the task) would likely hit first. Invoke at the start of any search where the task already implies a probable location. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-runtime/skills/cutit-progressive-widening) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-prompt-cache-preservation**: Catches an edit to a prompt's stable prefix (system instructions, fixed context) that silently invalidates a warm prompt cache for every call after it. Invoke before editing anything earlier in an assembled prompt than the newest per-call content. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-runtime/skills/cutit-prompt-cache-preservation) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-query-rewriting**: Catches the retry-loop pattern where a vague first-pass query returns weak results and triggers a second or third retrieval call — invoke before issuing a retrieval query built directly from raw user phrasing. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-tooling/skills/cutit-query-rewriting) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-relevance-filtering**: Catches carrying the full accumulated history into a step when only a fraction of it bears on that step — invoke before referencing "everything so far" to decide the next action. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-runtime/skills/cutit-relevance-filtering) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-rerank-before-stuff**: Catches paying to include irrelevant retrieved candidates in the prompt when they could have been scored and dropped first — invoke whenever multiple retrieval candidates are about to be inserted into context ("stuffed") before the model reasons over them. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-tooling/skills/cutit-rerank-before-stuff) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-response-length-budgeting**: Catches defaulting to a maximally thorough, multi-section answer regardless of how big the question actually is — invoke before drafting any response, to size it to the question first. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-quality/skills/cutit-response-length-budgeting) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-result-digestion**: Catches a sub-agent handing its full raw transcript, file dumps, or exploration log back to the parent instead of a compressed summary — the parent then re-reads all of it, doubling the cost of everything the sub-agent already processed. Invoke when a delegated agent is about to report its results back to its caller. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-agents/skills/cutit-result-digestion) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-result-memoization**: Catches recomputing a deterministic result (a build, a query, a parsed file, a computed diff) a second time in the same session when the inputs haven't changed. Invoke before re-running any tool call whose output you already have from earlier in this session. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-runtime/skills/cutit-result-memoization) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-retrieval-caching**: Catches re-running retrieval for a query the session already answered, or one close enough in meaning to reuse — invoke before issuing a retrieval call inside a loop that has run similar queries earlier in the same session. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-tooling/skills/cutit-retrieval-caching) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-retry-without-reload**: Catches re-sending context that already made it into a failed tool call — invoke right after any tool call errors out and before constructing the retry. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-tooling/skills/cutit-retry-without-reload) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-role-specialization**: Catches the pattern of giving every agent in a pipeline the same broad context and letting each one re-derive facts another agent already established. Invoke when defining agent roles or system prompts in a multi-agent pipeline. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-agents/skills/cutit-role-specialization) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-rolling-summary**: Catches keeping a long session's full turn-by-turn history live when a maintained running summary would carry the same load-bearing facts — invoke once a session has accumulated many resolved turns. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-runtime/skills/cutit-rolling-summary) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-root-cause-first-retry**: Catches the reflex of re-running a failed command or test unchanged and hoping for a different outcome, instead of reading the first failure closely enough to name why it happened. Invoke the moment a command fails and before issuing any retry. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-runtime/skills/cutit-root-cause-first-retry) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-sampled-eval-review**: Catches reading every failing transcript in an eval run when a representative sample would surface the same failure modes at a fraction of the tokens. Invoke when an eval run produces more failing cases than are needed to identify distinct failure patterns. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-quality/skills/cutit-sampled-eval-review) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-scratchpad-offloading**: Catches keeping large intermediate state (accumulated notes, partial results, a growing list) live in the prompt across many turns instead of writing it to a scratch file and reading back only what's needed — invoke whenever intermediate state needs to survive more than a couple of turns. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-runtime/skills/cutit-scratchpad-offloading) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-search-index-first**: Catches reaching straight for a live repo-wide grep/find when a cheaper prebuilt index, lockfile, manifest, or symbol table already answers the same question. Invoke before any raw search when the target is the kind of thing a project already indexes (dependency, symbol definition, route, exported API). ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-runtime/skills/cutit-search-index-first) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-search-result-triage**: Catches opening every hit from a search in full when only the top few are actually relevant to the task at hand. Invoke whenever a Grep/Glob/search tool returns more than a handful of results. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-runtime/skills/cutit-search-result-triage) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-selective-recall**: Catches a memory or history query returning the entire store when only the matching slice answers the question — invoke whenever querying any persistent history, log, or memory backend. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-runtime/skills/cutit-selective-recall) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-session-resumability**: Catches losing enough state that a long task must restart from scratch after an interruption — invoke at the start of any multi-phase task and after each phase completes, to checkpoint the minimum needed to resume. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-runtime/skills/cutit-session-resumability) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-shared-state-store**: Catches the pattern of re-transmitting the same state (full config, accumulated results, running context) in every message between agents instead of passing a reference to where it already lives. Invoke when an inter-agent message would otherwise repeat data already sent in a prior message. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-agents/skills/cutit-shared-state-store) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-single-pass-confidence**: Catches the pattern of shipping a fast, under-investigated answer and then paying for a full correction cycle once it's found wrong. Invoke before answering or generating a solution when a wrong first attempt would trigger re-reading the same context to fix it. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-quality/skills/cutit-single-pass-confidence) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-spawn-avoidance**: Catches the default instinct to reach for a sub-agent whenever a task looks even slightly involved, when the calling agent already has every tool and every piece of context needed to just do it directly. Invoke as the first check the instant you consider spawning any agent. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-agents/skills/cutit-spawn-avoidance) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-stale-context-pruning**: Catches letting an outdated version of something (a file since re-read, a plan since revised) linger in context alongside its replacement — invoke whenever a piece of context gets superseded by a fresher read or decision. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-runtime/skills/cutit-stale-context-pruning) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-state-diffing**: Catches rewriting an entire tracked state document (plan, todo list, progress file) from scratch each time one item changes, instead of patching just the change. Invoke whenever tracked state needs an update and the mechanism supports partial edits. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-runtime/skills/cutit-state-diffing) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-step-sizing**: Catches plans whose step granularity is miscalibrated against verification cost — either so fine-grained that checking each step costs more than the step itself, or so coarse that a failure requires re-tracing a huge span to isolate. Invoke when defining the grain of steps in a multi-step plan, before executing it. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-agents/skills/cutit-step-sizing) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-structured-output**: Catches generating flowing prose when the caller (a tool, a script, another agent) only ever consumes specific fields — invoke before writing a response that will be parsed, filtered, or forwarded rather than read as narrative. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-quality/skills/cutit-structured-output) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-subagent-scoping**: Catches handing a sub-agent an open-ended brief ("look into the auth system," "improve this module") that invites it to explore far beyond what's needed, instead of a closed task with a defined boundary and stopping condition. Invoke when writing the task description for a sub-agent you've decided to delegate to. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-agents/skills/cutit-subagent-scoping) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-summary-first**: Catches building up to an answer through a walkthrough of the investigation before stating the conclusion — invoke before structuring any response that has a clear bottom line. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-quality/skills/cutit-summary-first) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-system-prompt-diet**: Catches system prompts that accumulate instructions for tools, formats, or edge cases the current task never touches — invoke when writing or reviewing a system prompt that's grown large or covers many unrelated scenarios. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-tooling/skills/cutit-system-prompt-diet) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-targeted-verification**: Catches the reflex of re-running the whole test suite or re-reading every consumer of a module after a change whose blast radius is already known and small. Invoke right after a change when you can name exactly which files/functions/tests it could plausibly affect. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-quality/skills/cutit-targeted-verification) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-task-decomposition**: Catches the pattern of starting execution on a shallow plan, hitting a wrong assumption mid-task, and re-planning from scratch — each false start re-reads context you already paid for once. Invoke when breaking a non-trivial multi-step task into steps before executing any of them. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-agents/skills/cutit-task-decomposition) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-template-reuse**: Catches reformatting or regenerating a fixed prompt template's boilerplate on every call instead of reusing its cached prefix verbatim — invoke when a loop calls the same prompt template repeatedly with only small parts changing. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-tooling/skills/cutit-template-reuse) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-terse-tool-narration**: Catches announcing every routine tool call in a full sentence ("Now I'll read the file to check X") before making it — invoke before any expected, unsurprising tool call in a sequence. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-quality/skills/cutit-terse-tool-narration) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-todo-granularity**: Catches creating a tracked todo item for every trivial one-line action, where the overhead of adding, updating, and re-reading the item costs more tokens than the step itself. Invoke when building or expanding a task list for a multi-step job. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-runtime/skills/cutit-todo-granularity) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-tool-call-batching**: Catches issuing independent tool calls one at a time across separate turns, each round-trip re-stating the same surrounding context — invoke whenever you're about to make a tool call and can already see the next one coming. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-tooling/skills/cutit-tool-call-batching) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-tool-output-formatting**: Catches requesting prose or verbose formatting from a tool/subroutine when the agent itself is the only consumer of the result — invoke whenever a tool call's output feeds straight into another step rather than a human-facing reply. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-tooling/skills/cutit-tool-output-formatting) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-tool-result-capping**: Catches calling a tool with no limit/page size set, letting it return everything it can find — invoke before any search, list, or query call that supports a bound parameter. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-tooling/skills/cutit-tool-result-capping) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-tool-schema-pruning**: Catches carrying tool definitions the current task will never invoke — invoke when configuring an agent's toolset or noticing a long tool list where most entries are clearly out of scope for the task at hand. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-tooling/skills/cutit-tool-schema-pruning) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-tool-selection**: Catches reaching for a broad, general-purpose tool when a narrow one exists — invoke whenever more than one tool could answer the same call, since the broad one both costs more to invoke and returns more to read. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-tooling/skills/cutit-tool-selection) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-top-k-tuning**: Catches over-fetching in retrieval calls — pulling top-20 and filtering down in the prompt instead of asking for what's actually needed. Invoke when setting or defaulting a top-k/limit parameter on a retrieval or search call. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-tooling/skills/cutit-top-k-tuning) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-wakeup-scheduling**: Catches scheduling a fixed, over-frequent check-in cadence regardless of how fast the watched condition actually changes — invoke when setting up any recurring check, loop interval, or scheduled wake-up. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-runtime/skills/cutit-wakeup-scheduling) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **cutit-working-set-minimization**: Catches keeping files, variables, or state live in context long after the step that needed them has passed — invoke when moving on to a new step within a multi-step task. ([kh-bikash/cutit](https://github.com/kh-bikash/cutit/tree/HEAD/plugins/cutit-runtime/skills/cutit-working-set-minimization) · ★2 · 2 copies)
